-- ============================================================================================
-- WorkNest: every database change of 21 Oct 2026, in one script for the LIVE database.
-- Run it connected to the live database, as ONE statement in DBeaver: select all (Cmd+A), then Ctrl+Enter.
--
-- Steps (each is the script of the same name in WN_APIs/Database, unchanged, in its own transaction):
--    1. CORE_01  test-database (SAC400.) references removed from WN_ procedures
--    2. CUS_01   customer columns as on test
--    3. CUS_02   WN_Customers_Insert with 16 parameters (create customer fix)
--    4. OB_01    One Bill: columns, F-7 codes, invoice procedures
--    5. OB_03    One Bill: payment notifications table
--    6. OB_04    One Bill: WN_OneBill_RecordPayment with @StatusCode
--    7. RC_01    monthly split of invoices (WN_FeeReceipt_MonthPlan)
--    8. RC_02    one fee receipt per month, vouchers, void
--    9. RC_03    CEO dashboard by month shares
--   10. BK_11    booking statuses from WN_BookingStatuses (last: stops if that table differs)
--
-- Every step is safe to run again (a step that is already in place changes nothing). If a step fails, that step is
-- rolled back, the script stops and says which step; the steps before it stay done. Fix the cause and run it again.
-- Not included: OB_02 (only for the test database) and BK_10 (replaced by BK_11).
-- ============================================================================================
SET XACT_ABORT ON;
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON;
SET ANSI_NULLS ON;
DECLARE @Step NVARCHAR(200) = N'';
BEGIN TRY
    -- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    -- Step 1: CORE_01  test-database (SAC400.) references removed from WN_ procedures   (Core/CORE_01_remove_test_db_references_LIVE.txt)
    SET @Step = N'1: CORE_01  test-database (SAC400.) references removed from WN_ procedures';
    EXEC(N'-- ============================================================================================
-- LIVE fix: procedures / views / functions / triggers that name the TEST database (SAC400.dbo.xxx) instead of their own
-- database. On live they fail with "Invalid object name ''SAC400.dbo.WN_Invoices''" (seen in the live API log from the
-- challan access suspension job: dbo.WN_HIK_AccessSuspension_Run; also _Extend, _GetByBookingDetail, _SetApplied).
--
-- For every such WorkNest object (WN_*, TR_WN_*) in THIS database, the SAC400 prefix is removed (SAC400.dbo.X / [SAC400].[dbo].X -> dbo.X) and
-- the object is created again from its own current code, nothing else changed. Objects without a SAC400 reference are
-- not touched.
--
-- Run against the LIVE database (WorkNestAEO) as ONE statement in DBeaver: select all (Cmd+A), then Ctrl+Enter.
-- One transaction: if any object fails to compile, nothing is changed and the error names it. Safe to run again
-- (the second run finds nothing to fix). The result lists every object fixed.
-- ============================================================================================
SET XACT_ABORT ON;
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON;
SET ANSI_NULLS ON;
SET NOCOUNT ON;

IF DB_NAME() = N''SAC400''
    THROW 50001, N''This is the test database (SAC400): its own SAC400 references are valid. Run this on the live database.'', 1;

DECLARE @Fix TABLE (Ord INT IDENTITY(1, 1), ObjectId INT, ObjectName NVARCHAR(300), TypeDesc NVARCHAR(60),
                    Kind NVARCHAR(20), Definition NVARCHAR(MAX), Refs INT);

INSERT INTO @Fix (ObjectId, ObjectName, TypeDesc, Kind, Definition, Refs)
SELECT o.object_id,
       QUOTENAME(SCHEMA_NAME(o.schema_id)) + N''.'' + QUOTENAME(o.name),
       o.type_desc,
       CASE o.type WHEN ''P'' THEN N''PROCEDURE'' WHEN ''V'' THEN N''VIEW'' WHEN ''TR'' THEN N''TRIGGER'' ELSE N''FUNCTION'' END,
       m.definition,
       (LEN(m.definition) - LEN(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(m.definition, N''[SAC400].'', N''#''), N''SAC400.'', N''#''), N''#'', N''''))) -- rough count
  FROM sys.sql_modules m
  JOIN sys.objects o ON o.object_id = m.object_id
 WHERE o.type IN (''P'', ''V'', ''TR'', ''FN'', ''IF'', ''TF'')
   AND (o.name LIKE N''WN[_]%'' OR o.name LIKE N''TR[_]WN[_]%'')   -- WorkNest objects only (accounting objects may use SAC400 on purpose)
   AND (m.definition LIKE N''%SAC400.dbo.%'' OR m.definition LIKE N''%SAC400..%''
        OR m.definition LIKE N''%[[]SAC400].[[]dbo].%'' OR m.definition LIKE N''%[[]SAC400].dbo.%'');

BEGIN TRY
    BEGIN TRANSACTION;

    DECLARE @i INT = 1, @n INT = (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM @Fix), @Name NVARCHAR(300), @Kind NVARCHAR(20), @Def NVARCHAR(MAX);
    WHILE @i <= @n
    BEGIN
        SELECT @Name = ObjectName, @Kind = Kind, @Def = Definition FROM @Fix WHERE Ord = @i;

        SET @Def = REPLACE(@Def, N''[SAC400].[dbo].'', N''[dbo].'');
        SET @Def = REPLACE(@Def, N''[SAC400].dbo.'', N''dbo.'');
        SET @Def = REPLACE(@Def, N''SAC400.dbo.'', N''dbo.'');
        SET @Def = REPLACE(@Def, N''SAC400..'', N''dbo.'');

        -- Same object, same code, without the test-database prefix (dropped and created again in this transaction).
        EXEC(N''DROP '' + @Kind + N'' '' + @Name + N'';'');
        EXEC(@Def);

        SET @i += 1;
    END;

    COMMIT TRANSACTION;

    IF @n = 0
        SELECT N''Nothing to fix: no object in '' + DB_NAME() + N'' refers to SAC400.'' AS Result;
    ELSE
        SELECT ObjectName, TypeDesc, N''Fixed: SAC400 prefix removed'' AS Result FROM @Fix ORDER BY Ord;
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
    IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0 ROLLBACK TRANSACTION;
    DECLARE @Err NVARCHAR(2000) = N''Nothing was changed. Failed on '' + ISNULL(@Name, N''?'') + N'': '' + ERROR_MESSAGE();
    THROW 50002, @Err, 1;
END CATCH;
');

    -- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    -- Step 2: CUS_01   customer columns as on test   (Customer/CUS_01_customers_match_test.txt)
    SET @Step = N'2: CUS_01   customer columns as on test';
    EXEC(N'-- (USE removed: runs on the database you are connected to)
-- ============================================================================================
-- Brings dbo.WN_Customers on this database up to the design used by the test database
-- (SAC400 on 119.159.146.178, rebuilt 2026-10-12) WITHOUT deleting anything.
--
-- Adds only what is missing: columns, column defaults, the unique keys on Code / Email / UserId and
-- the UserId -> WN_Users link. Existing columns, data, keys and links are never changed or dropped.
-- A unique key or link is skipped (and reported) when existing data would break it, e.g. two
-- customers with the same email. Columns that the test database requires (NOT NULL) but that have no
-- default are added as nullable here, so existing rows stay valid.
-- Not included: the audit trigger TR_WN_Customers_Audit (needs dbo.WN_AuditLog on this database).
--
-- Run against the LIVE database as ONE script in DBeaver (select all, execute). Safe to run again.
-- The last result lists every step: Added / Already there / Skipped (with the reason).
-- ============================================================================================
SET NOCOUNT ON;

IF OBJECT_ID(N''dbo.WN_Customers'', N''U'') IS NULL
BEGIN
    SELECT N''dbo.WN_Customers was not found on this database. Nothing changed.'' AS Result;
    RETURN;
END

DECLARE @Log TABLE (Step INT IDENTITY(1,1), Item NVARCHAR(200), Result NVARCHAR(400));
DECLARE @Cols TABLE (Ord INT, Name SYSNAME, Def NVARCHAR(400), DefaultName SYSNAME NULL, DefaultExpr NVARCHAR(100) NULL);

-- Same columns, types and sizes as the test database (sizes in characters).
INSERT INTO @Cols (Ord, Name, Def, DefaultName, DefaultExpr) VALUES
 ( 1, N''IdGUID'',                       N''UNIQUEIDENTIFIER NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_WN_Customers_IdGUID DEFAULT NEWID()'', N''DF_WN_Customers_IdGUID'',  N''NEWID()''),
 ( 2, N''Code'',                         N''VARCHAR(7) NULL'',        NULL, NULL),
 ( 3, N''FirstName'',                    N''NVARCHAR(100) NULL'',     NULL, NULL),
 ( 4, N''LastName'',                     N''NVARCHAR(100) NULL'',     NULL, NULL),
 ( 5, N''Email'',                        N''NVARCHAR(255) NULL'',     NULL, NULL),
 ( 6, N''PhoneNumber'',                  N''NVARCHAR(50) NULL'',      NULL, NULL),
 ( 7, N''CnicOrPassport'',               N''NVARCHAR(50) NULL'',      NULL, NULL),
 ( 8, N''Address'',                      N''NVARCHAR(500) NULL'',     NULL, NULL),
 ( 9, N''CityId'',                       N''INT NULL'',               NULL, NULL),
 (10, N''IsActive'',                     N''BIT NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_WN_Customers_IsActive DEFAULT 1'',            N''DF_WN_Customers_IsActive'',  N''1''),
 (11, N''Notes'',                        N''NVARCHAR(1000) NULL'',    NULL, NULL),
 (12, N''CreatedAt'',                    N''DATETIME NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_WN_Customers_CreatedAt DEFAULT GETDATE()'', N''DF_WN_Customers_CreatedAt'', N''GETDATE()''),
 (13, N''UpdatedAt'',                    N''DATETIME NULL'',          NULL, NULL),
 (14, N''CreatedBy'',                    N''NVARCHAR(255) NULL'',     NULL, NULL),
 (15, N''UserId'',                       N''INT NULL'',               NULL, NULL),
 (16, N''Company'',                      N''NVARCHAR(255) NULL'',     NULL, NULL),
 (17, N''NTN'',                          N''NVARCHAR(50) NULL'',      NULL, NULL),
 (18, N''SecpRegistrationNo'',           N''NVARCHAR(50) NULL'',      NULL, NULL),
 (19, N''CustomerTypeId'',               N''INT NULL'',               NULL, NULL),
 (20, N''CountryId'',                    N''INT NULL'',               NULL, NULL),
 (21, N''IdentificationDocumentTypeId'', N''INT NULL'',               NULL, NULL),
 (22, N''UpdatedById'',                  N''INT NULL'',               NULL, NULL);

DECLARE @Ord INT = 0, @Name SYSNAME, @Def NVARCHAR(400), @DfName SYSNAME, @DfExpr NVARCHAR(100), @Sql NVARCHAR(MAX);

-- 1. Columns (and the defaults of IdGUID / IsActive / CreatedAt) -------------------------------
WHILE 1 = 1
BEGIN
    SELECT TOP 1 @Ord = Ord, @Name = Name, @Def = Def, @DfName = DefaultName, @DfExpr = DefaultExpr
      FROM @Cols WHERE Ord > @Ord ORDER BY Ord;
    IF @@ROWCOUNT = 0 BREAK;

    IF COL_LENGTH(N''dbo.WN_Customers'', @Name) IS NULL
    BEGIN
        SET @Sql = N''ALTER TABLE dbo.WN_Customers ADD '' + QUOTENAME(@Name) + N'' '' + @Def + N'';'';
        EXEC sys.sp_executesql @Sql;
        INSERT INTO @Log (Item, Result) VALUES (N''Column '' + @Name, N''Added'');
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        INSERT INTO @Log (Item, Result) VALUES (N''Column '' + @Name, N''Already there (not changed)'');
        IF @DfName IS NOT NULL AND NOT EXISTS (
            SELECT 1 FROM sys.default_constraints dc
             WHERE dc.parent_object_id = OBJECT_ID(N''dbo.WN_Customers'')
               AND dc.parent_column_id = COLUMNPROPERTY(OBJECT_ID(N''dbo.WN_Customers''), @Name, ''ColumnId''))
        BEGIN
            SET @Sql = N''ALTER TABLE dbo.WN_Customers ADD CONSTRAINT '' + QUOTENAME(@DfName)
                     + N'' DEFAULT '' + @DfExpr + N'' FOR '' + QUOTENAME(@Name) + N'';'';
            EXEC sys.sp_executesql @Sql;
            INSERT INTO @Log (Item, Result) VALUES (N''Default for '' + @Name, N''Added'');
        END
    END
END

-- 2. Unique keys on Code and Email (only when no other unique key covers the column and the data has no
--    duplicates; a plain unique key allows only one empty value, so empty values count as duplicates).
DECLARE @Uq TABLE (Name SYSNAME, Col SYSNAME);
INSERT INTO @Uq VALUES (N''UQ_WN_Customers_Code'', N''Code''), (N''UQ_WN_Customers_Email'', N''Email'');
DECLARE @UqName SYSNAME, @UqCol SYSNAME, @Dups INT;
DECLARE uq CURSOR LOCAL FAST_FORWARD FOR SELECT Name, Col FROM @Uq;
OPEN uq;
FETCH NEXT FROM uq INTO @UqName, @UqCol;
WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
BEGIN
    IF EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM sys.indexes i
          JOIN sys.index_columns ic ON ic.object_id = i.object_id AND ic.index_id = i.index_id
         WHERE i.object_id = OBJECT_ID(N''dbo.WN_Customers'') AND i.is_unique = 1
           AND COL_NAME(ic.object_id, ic.column_id) = @UqCol
           AND (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM sys.index_columns x WHERE x.object_id = i.object_id AND x.index_id = i.index_id AND x.is_included_column = 0) = 1)
        INSERT INTO @Log (Item, Result) VALUES (N''Unique key on '' + @UqCol, N''Already there (not changed)'');
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SET @Sql = N''SELECT @n = COUNT(*) FROM (SELECT '' + QUOTENAME(@UqCol) + N'' AS v FROM dbo.WN_Customers GROUP BY ''
                 + QUOTENAME(@UqCol) + N'' HAVING COUNT(*) > 1) d;'';
        EXEC sys.sp_executesql @Sql, N''@n INT OUTPUT'', @n = @Dups OUTPUT;
        IF @Dups > 0
            INSERT INTO @Log (Item, Result) VALUES (N''Unique key on '' + @UqCol,
                N''Skipped: '' + CAST(@Dups AS NVARCHAR(10)) + N'' value(s) appear more than once (or are empty more than once). Clean these first.'');
        ELSE
        BEGIN
            SET @Sql = N''ALTER TABLE dbo.WN_Customers ADD CONSTRAINT '' + QUOTENAME(@UqName) + N'' UNIQUE ('' + QUOTENAME(@UqCol) + N'');'';
            EXEC sys.sp_executesql @Sql;
            INSERT INTO @Log (Item, Result) VALUES (N''Unique key on '' + @UqCol, N''Added'');
        END
    END
    FETCH NEXT FROM uq INTO @UqName, @UqCol;
END
CLOSE uq; DEALLOCATE uq;

-- 3. One customer per user account (unique UserId, empty values allowed) ------------------------
IF EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM sys.indexes i
      JOIN sys.index_columns ic ON ic.object_id = i.object_id AND ic.index_id = i.index_id
     WHERE i.object_id = OBJECT_ID(N''dbo.WN_Customers'') AND i.is_unique = 1
       AND COL_NAME(ic.object_id, ic.column_id) = N''UserId'')
    INSERT INTO @Log (Item, Result) VALUES (N''Unique key on UserId'', N''Already there (not changed)'');
ELSE
BEGIN
    EXEC sys.sp_executesql
        N''SELECT @n = COUNT(*) FROM (SELECT UserId FROM dbo.WN_Customers WHERE UserId IS NOT NULL GROUP BY UserId HAVING COUNT(*) > 1) d;'',
        N''@n INT OUTPUT'', @n = @Dups OUTPUT;
    IF @Dups > 0
        INSERT INTO @Log (Item, Result) VALUES (N''Unique key on UserId'',
            N''Skipped: '' + CAST(@Dups AS NVARCHAR(10)) + N'' user account(s) have more than one customer record. Clean these first.'');
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        EXEC sys.sp_executesql N''CREATE UNIQUE NONCLUSTERED INDEX UQ_WN_Customers_UserId ON dbo.WN_Customers (UserId) WHERE UserId IS NOT NULL;'';
        INSERT INTO @Log (Item, Result) VALUES (N''Unique key on UserId'', N''Added'');
    END
END

-- 4. Link UserId -> WN_Users (only when every filled-in UserId exists in WN_Users) ---------------
IF EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM sys.foreign_key_columns fc
     WHERE fc.parent_object_id = OBJECT_ID(N''dbo.WN_Customers'')
       AND COL_NAME(fc.parent_object_id, fc.parent_column_id) = N''UserId''
       AND fc.referenced_object_id = OBJECT_ID(N''dbo.WN_Users''))
    INSERT INTO @Log (Item, Result) VALUES (N''Link UserId -> WN_Users'', N''Already there (not changed)'');
ELSE
BEGIN
    EXEC sys.sp_executesql
        N''SELECT @n = COUNT(*) FROM dbo.WN_Customers c WHERE c.UserId IS NOT NULL AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM dbo.WN_Users u WHERE u.Id = c.UserId);'',
        N''@n INT OUTPUT'', @n = @Dups OUTPUT;
    IF @Dups > 0
        INSERT INTO @Log (Item, Result) VALUES (N''Link UserId -> WN_Users'',
            N''Skipped: '' + CAST(@Dups AS NVARCHAR(10)) + N'' customer(s) point to a user account that does not exist.'');
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        EXEC sys.sp_executesql N''ALTER TABLE dbo.WN_Customers WITH CHECK ADD CONSTRAINT FK_WN_Customers_User FOREIGN KEY (UserId) REFERENCES dbo.WN_Users (Id);'';
        INSERT INTO @Log (Item, Result) VALUES (N''Link UserId -> WN_Users'', N''Added'');
    END
END

SELECT Step, Item, Result FROM @Log ORDER BY Step;
SELECT COUNT(*) AS CustomersAfter, DB_NAME() AS DatabaseName FROM dbo.WN_Customers;
');

    -- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    -- Step 3: CUS_02   WN_Customers_Insert with 16 parameters (create customer fix)   (Customer/CUS_02_customers_insert_live.txt)
    SET @Step = N'3: CUS_02   WN_Customers_Insert with 16 parameters (create customer fix)';
    EXEC(N'-- ============================================================================================
-- Fix for "Procedure or function WN_Customers_Insert has too many arguments specified" on live
-- (creating a customer in the CMS, and the first Google sign-in of a new customer).
--
-- The API sends 16 parameters to dbo.WN_Customers_Insert (DbRepository.CreateCustomerAsync); the live procedure
-- is an older version with fewer. This installs the same procedure as the test database (SAC400).
--
-- Customer code: on the test database WN_Customers.Code is a computed column (''WN'' + Id padded to 5 digits, e.g.
-- WN00088). Where Code is a plain column (live), the procedure fills it right after the insert with that same formula.
-- The script stops if an existing code could clash with a code made that way (a WN code above the highest Id).
--
-- Needs the WN_Customers columns NTN, SecpRegistrationNo, CustomerTypeId, CountryId, IdentificationDocumentTypeId:
-- run CUS_01_customers_match_test.txt first if they are missing (this script stops and says so).
--
-- Run against the LIVE database as ONE statement in DBeaver: select all (Cmd+A), then Ctrl+Enter. No GO / USE needed.
-- One transaction: if anything fails, nothing is changed. Safe to run again (CREATE OR ALTER).
-- Before running, keep the current version if you want to compare: EXEC sp_helptext ''dbo.WN_Customers_Insert'';
-- ============================================================================================
SET XACT_ABORT ON;
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON;
SET ANSI_NULLS ON;
BEGIN TRY
    DECLARE @Missing NVARCHAR(400) = STUFF((
        SELECT N'', '' + c.Name
          FROM (VALUES (N''NTN''), (N''SecpRegistrationNo''), (N''CustomerTypeId''), (N''CountryId''), (N''IdentificationDocumentTypeId'')) c(Name)
         WHERE COL_LENGTH(''dbo.WN_Customers'', c.Name) IS NULL
           FOR XML PATH('''')), 1, 2, N'''');
    IF @Missing IS NOT NULL
        THROW 50001, N''dbo.WN_Customers is missing columns needed by the new procedure. Run CUS_01_customers_match_test.txt first.'', 1;

    DECLARE @CodeIsComputed BIT = ISNULL(COLUMNPROPERTY(OBJECT_ID(''dbo.WN_Customers''), ''Code'', ''IsComputed''), 0);
    IF @CodeIsComputed = 0
    BEGIN
        DECLARE @MaxId INT, @Clashes INT;
        EXEC sp_executesql N''SELECT @m = ISNULL(MAX(Id), 0) FROM dbo.WN_Customers;'', N''@m INT OUTPUT'', @m = @MaxId OUTPUT;
        EXEC sp_executesql N''SELECT @n = COUNT(*) FROM dbo.WN_Customers
                              WHERE Code LIKE ''''WN[0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9]'''' AND TRY_CAST(SUBSTRING(Code, 3, 5) AS INT) > @max;'',
             N''@max INT, @n INT OUTPUT'', @max = @MaxId, @n = @Clashes OUTPUT;
        IF @Clashes > 0
            THROW 50002, N''Some existing customer codes are higher than the highest customer Id, so new codes could clash. Nothing changed: send these rows to the developer: SELECT Id, Code FROM dbo.WN_Customers ORDER BY Id DESC;'', 1;
    END;

    -- The code line of the procedure: nothing when Code is computed, else fill it with the test formula.
    DECLARE @CodeLine NVARCHAR(400) = CASE WHEN @CodeIsComputed = 1 THEN N'''' ELSE
        N''UPDATE dbo.WN_Customers SET Code = ''''WN'''' + RIGHT(''''00000'''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @NewId), 5) WHERE Id = @NewId AND (Code IS NULL OR Code = '''''''');'' END;

    BEGIN TRANSACTION;

    DECLARE @Sql NVARCHAR(MAX) = N''CREATE OR ALTER PROCEDURE dbo.WN_Customers_Insert
    @FirstName                   NVARCHAR(100),
    @LastName                    NVARCHAR(100)  = NULL,
    @Email                       NVARCHAR(255),
    @PhoneNumber                 NVARCHAR(50)   = NULL,
    @CnicOrPassport              NVARCHAR(50)   = NULL,
    @Address                     NVARCHAR(500)  = NULL,
    @CityId                      INT            = NULL,
    @Notes                       NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
    @CreatedBy                   NVARCHAR(255)  = NULL,
    @UserId                      INT            = NULL,
    @Company                     NVARCHAR(255)  = NULL,
    @NTN                         NVARCHAR(100)  = NULL,
    @SecpRegistrationNo          NVARCHAR(100)  = NULL,
    @CustomerTypeId              NVARCHAR(225)  = NULL,
    @CountryId                   INT            = NULL,
    @IdentificationDocumentTypeId NVARCHAR(225) = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    -- Treat 0 or negative foreign keys / IDs as NULL
    IF @CountryId IS NULL OR @CountryId <= 0 SET @CountryId = NULL;
    IF @CityId IS NULL OR @CityId <= 0 SET @CityId = NULL;
    IF @UserId IS NULL OR @UserId <= 0 SET @UserId = NULL;

    -- Treat empty strings and ''''0'''' as NULL for string / optional fields
    IF @CustomerTypeId IS NULL OR LTRIM(RTRIM(@CustomerTypeId)) IN ('''''''', ''''0'''') SET @CustomerTypeId = NULL;
    IF @IdentificationDocumentTypeId IS NULL OR LTRIM(RTRIM(@IdentificationDocumentTypeId)) IN ('''''''', ''''0'''') SET @IdentificationDocumentTypeId = NULL;
    IF LTRIM(RTRIM(@Company)) = '''''''' SET @Company = NULL;
    IF LTRIM(RTRIM(@LastName)) = '''''''' SET @LastName = NULL;
    IF LTRIM(RTRIM(@PhoneNumber)) = '''''''' SET @PhoneNumber = NULL;
    IF LTRIM(RTRIM(@CnicOrPassport)) = '''''''' SET @CnicOrPassport = NULL;
    IF LTRIM(RTRIM(@Address)) = '''''''' SET @Address = NULL;
    IF LTRIM(RTRIM(@Notes)) = '''''''' SET @Notes = NULL;
    IF LTRIM(RTRIM(@CreatedBy)) = '''''''' SET @CreatedBy = NULL;
    IF LTRIM(RTRIM(@NTN)) = '''''''' SET @NTN = NULL;
    IF LTRIM(RTRIM(@SecpRegistrationNo)) = '''''''' SET @SecpRegistrationNo = NULL;

    INSERT INTO dbo.WN_Customers
    (
        FirstName, LastName, Company, Email, PhoneNumber, CnicOrPassport, Address, CityId, Notes, CreatedBy, UserId,
        NTN, SecpRegistrationNo, CustomerTypeId, CountryId, IdentificationDocumentTypeId
    )
    VALUES
    (
        @FirstName, @LastName, @Company, @Email, @PhoneNumber, @CnicOrPassport, @Address, @CityId, @Notes, @CreatedBy, @UserId,
        @NTN, @SecpRegistrationNo, TRY_CAST(@CustomerTypeId AS INT), @CountryId, TRY_CAST(@IdentificationDocumentTypeId AS INT)
    );

    DECLARE @NewId INT = CAST(SCOPE_IDENTITY() AS INT);
    '' + @CodeLine + N''

    SELECT @NewId AS ID;
END;'';
    EXEC(@Sql);

    COMMIT TRANSACTION;

    -- Check: 16 parameters, as the API sends.
    SELECT COUNT(*) AS ParameterCount,
           CASE WHEN @CodeIsComputed = 1 THEN N''Code is computed (as on test).'' ELSE N''Code is filled by the procedure (WN + Id).'' END AS CustomerCode,
           N''dbo.WN_Customers_Insert updated (expected 16 parameters).'' AS Result
      FROM sys.parameters WHERE object_id = OBJECT_ID(''dbo.WN_Customers_Insert'');
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
    IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0 ROLLBACK TRANSACTION;
    THROW;
END CATCH;
');

    -- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    -- Step 4: OB_01    One Bill: columns, F-7 codes, invoice procedures   (OneBill/OB_01_one_bill.txt)
    SET @Step = N'4: OB_01    One Bill: columns, F-7 codes, invoice procedures';
    EXEC(N'-- ============================================================================================
-- One Bill (EzPay / 1-Link): invoices of a location that has a 1-Link biller code are also created on EzPay, and the
-- 1-Link consumer number ("One Bill number") customers pay with is kept on the invoice.
-- Run as ONE statement in DBeaver: select all (Cmd+A), then Ctrl+Enter. No GO / USE needed.
-- One transaction: if anything fails, nothing is changed. Safe to run again (columns added only when missing,
-- CREATE OR ALTER for the procedures; the F-7 codes are only set while still empty).
--
-- Adds:
--   WN_Locations.OneBillBillerCode / OneBillPrefix / OneBillFrom  - which location uses One Bill, and from when.
--   WN_Invoices.OneBill*  / EzPay*                                  - the One Bill number and the EzPay result.
--   WN_OneBill_ClaimInvoice, WN_OneBill_SetResult, WN_OneBill_GetPending, WN_OneBill_GetByInvoice.
--
-- A location uses One Bill when both OneBillBillerCode and OneBillPrefix are set. For an invoice of that location:
--   ref sent to EzPay (concat_vendor_invoice_one_bill_ref) = OneBillBillerCode + invoice Id padded to 8 digits
--   One Bill number the customer pays                      = OneBillPrefix     + invoice Id padded to 8 digits
--   e.g. invoice 4162 at F-7: ref 103400004162, One Bill number 101340103400004162.
-- Only invoices created on/after OneBillFrom (UTC, like WN_Invoices.CreatedOn) are sent (older invoices are left alone).
-- ============================================================================================
SET XACT_ABORT ON;
BEGIN TRY
    BEGIN TRANSACTION;

    -- 1. Locations: 1-Link biller code, 1-Link prefix, start date.
    IF COL_LENGTH(''dbo.WN_Locations'', ''OneBillBillerCode'') IS NULL
        EXEC(N''ALTER TABLE dbo.WN_Locations ADD OneBillBillerCode VARCHAR(10) NULL;'');
    IF COL_LENGTH(''dbo.WN_Locations'', ''OneBillPrefix'') IS NULL
        EXEC(N''ALTER TABLE dbo.WN_Locations ADD OneBillPrefix VARCHAR(20) NULL;'');
    IF COL_LENGTH(''dbo.WN_Locations'', ''OneBillFrom'') IS NULL
        EXEC(N''ALTER TABLE dbo.WN_Locations ADD OneBillFrom DATETIME NULL;'');

    -- 2. Invoices: One Bill number and the EzPay result.
    --    OneBillStatus: NULL = not sent, 1 = being sent, 2 = created on EzPay, 3 = failed (retried until OneBillAttempts = 5).
    IF COL_LENGTH(''dbo.WN_Invoices'', ''OneBillRef'') IS NULL
        EXEC(N''ALTER TABLE dbo.WN_Invoices ADD OneBillRef VARCHAR(20) NULL;'');
    IF COL_LENGTH(''dbo.WN_Invoices'', ''OneBillNumber'') IS NULL
        EXEC(N''ALTER TABLE dbo.WN_Invoices ADD OneBillNumber VARCHAR(30) NULL;'');
    IF COL_LENGTH(''dbo.WN_Invoices'', ''OneBillStatus'') IS NULL
        EXEC(N''ALTER TABLE dbo.WN_Invoices ADD OneBillStatus TINYINT NULL;'');
    IF COL_LENGTH(''dbo.WN_Invoices'', ''OneBillAttempts'') IS NULL
        EXEC(N''ALTER TABLE dbo.WN_Invoices ADD OneBillAttempts INT NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_WN_Invoices_OneBillAttempts DEFAULT (0);'');
    IF COL_LENGTH(''dbo.WN_Invoices'', ''OneBillError'') IS NULL
        EXEC(N''ALTER TABLE dbo.WN_Invoices ADD OneBillError NVARCHAR(500) NULL;'');
    IF COL_LENGTH(''dbo.WN_Invoices'', ''OneBillClaimedOn'') IS NULL
        EXEC(N''ALTER TABLE dbo.WN_Invoices ADD OneBillClaimedOn DATETIME NULL;'');
    IF COL_LENGTH(''dbo.WN_Invoices'', ''OneBillCreatedOn'') IS NULL
        EXEC(N''ALTER TABLE dbo.WN_Invoices ADD OneBillCreatedOn DATETIME NULL;'');
    IF COL_LENGTH(''dbo.WN_Invoices'', ''EzPayInvoiceId'') IS NULL
        EXEC(N''ALTER TABLE dbo.WN_Invoices ADD EzPayInvoiceId BIGINT NULL;'');
    IF COL_LENGTH(''dbo.WN_Invoices'', ''EzPayTranAuthId'') IS NULL
        EXEC(N''ALTER TABLE dbo.WN_Invoices ADD EzPayTranAuthId BIGINT NULL;'');
    -- No index on OneBillRef: it is the invoice Id with the biller code in front, so it is unique already (and a filtered
    -- index would make older procedures that write WN_Invoices fail when they were created with QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF).

    -- 3. F-7 (WN_Locations.Id = 2): biller code 1034, 1-Link prefix 1013401034, from now on. Only set while empty.
    --    OneBillFrom is UTC, like WN_Invoices.CreatedOn (WN_Invoices_Insert writes SYSUTCDATETIME()).
    EXEC(N''UPDATE dbo.WN_Locations
              SET OneBillBillerCode = ''''1034'''', OneBillPrefix = ''''1013401034'''', OneBillFrom = SYSUTCDATETIME()
            WHERE Id = 2 AND OneBillBillerCode IS NULL AND OneBillPrefix IS NULL;'');

    -- 4. Claims an invoice for sending to EzPay and answers its data.
    --    Outcome: Claimed (send it; the row has the data), Done (already created; OneBillNumber), Busy (being sent
    --    elsewhere), NotApplicable (no One Bill location, too old, paid, deleted, zero, or attempts used up).
    EXEC(N''CREATE OR ALTER PROCEDURE dbo.WN_OneBill_ClaimInvoice
    @InvoiceId    INT,
    @MaxAttempts  INT = 5,
    @StaleMinutes INT = 10
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
    SET XACT_ABORT ON;

    DECLARE @Outcome VARCHAR(20), @Status TINYINT, @Attempts INT, @ClaimedOn DATETIME, @Number VARCHAR(30),
            @BillerCode VARCHAR(10), @Prefix VARCHAR(20), @From DATETIME, @CreatedOn DATETIME,
            @StatusId INT, @Grand DECIMAL(18, 2), @Paid DECIMAL(18, 2), @LocationId INT;

    BEGIN TRANSACTION;

    SELECT @Status = i.OneBillStatus, @Attempts = i.OneBillAttempts, @ClaimedOn = i.OneBillClaimedOn,
           @Number = i.OneBillNumber, @CreatedOn = i.CreatedOn, @StatusId = i.StatusId,
           @Grand = ISNULL(i.GrandTotal, 0), @Paid = ISNULL(i.PaidTotal, 0),
           @LocationId = COALESCE(ci.LocationId, s.LocationId, u.LocationId)
      FROM dbo.WN_Invoices i WITH (UPDLOCK, HOLDLOCK)
      LEFT JOIN dbo.WN_CustomInvoices ci WITH (NOLOCK) ON ci.InvoiceId = i.Id
      LEFT JOIN dbo.WN_Bookings b WITH (NOLOCK) ON b.Id = i.BookingId
      LEFT JOIN dbo.WN_Spaces s WITH (NOLOCK) ON s.Id = b.SpaceId
      LEFT JOIN dbo.WN_Users u WITH (NOLOCK) ON u.Id = i.UserId
     WHERE i.Id = @InvoiceId;

    SELECT @BillerCode = NULLIF(LTRIM(RTRIM(l.OneBillBillerCode)), ''''''''), @Prefix = NULLIF(LTRIM(RTRIM(l.OneBillPrefix)), ''''''''),
           @From = l.OneBillFrom
      FROM dbo.WN_Locations l WITH (NOLOCK)
     WHERE l.Id = @LocationId;

    IF @@ROWCOUNT = 0 OR @BillerCode IS NULL OR @Prefix IS NULL OR @CreatedOn IS NULL
       OR (@From IS NOT NULL AND @CreatedOn < @From) OR @InvoiceId > 99999999
        SET @Outcome = ''''NotApplicable'''';
    ELSE IF @Status = 2
        SET @Outcome = ''''Done'''';
    ELSE IF ISNULL(@StatusId, 0) = 40 OR @Grand <= 0 OR @Paid >= @Grand OR @Attempts >= @MaxAttempts
        SET @Outcome = ''''NotApplicable'''';
    ELSE IF @Status = 1 AND @ClaimedOn > DATEADD(MINUTE, -@StaleMinutes, GETDATE())
        SET @Outcome = ''''Busy'''';
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SET @Outcome = ''''Claimed'''';
        UPDATE dbo.WN_Invoices
           SET OneBillStatus = 1,
               OneBillClaimedOn = GETDATE(),
               OneBillAttempts = OneBillAttempts + 1,
               OneBillRef = @BillerCode + RIGHT(REPLICATE(''''0'''', 8) + CAST(@InvoiceId AS VARCHAR(8)), 8),
               OneBillNumber = @Prefix + RIGHT(REPLICATE(''''0'''', 8) + CAST(@InvoiceId AS VARCHAR(8)), 8)
         WHERE Id = @InvoiceId;
    END;

    COMMIT TRANSACTION;

    IF @Outcome <> ''''Claimed''''
    BEGIN
        SELECT @Outcome AS Outcome, @InvoiceId AS InvoiceId, CASE WHEN @Outcome = ''''Done'''' THEN @Number END AS OneBillNumber;
        RETURN;
    END;

    SELECT TOP 1
        @Outcome AS Outcome,
        i.Id AS InvoiceId,
        i.InvoiceNumber,
        i.OneBillRef,
        i.OneBillNumber,
        CAST(i.GrandTotal - ISNULL(i.PaidTotal, 0) AS DECIMAL(18, 2)) AS Amount,
        i.DueOn,
        i.BillingPeriodStart,
        i.BillingPeriodEnd,
        COALESCE(NULLIF(LTRIM(RTRIM(c.Code)), ''''''''), NULLIF(LTRIM(RTRIM(b.CustomerCode)), ''''''''), ''''C'''' + CAST(i.UserId AS VARCHAR(12))) AS RegistrationNumber,
        COALESCE(NULLIF(LTRIM(RTRIM(c.Company)), ''''''''), NULLIF(LTRIM(RTRIM(ISNULL(c.FirstName, '''''''') + '''' '''' + ISNULL(c.LastName, ''''''''))), ''''''''),
                 NULLIF(LTRIM(RTRIM(u.Name)), ''''''''), ''''WorkNest Customer'''') AS CustomerName,
        COALESCE(NULLIF(LTRIM(RTRIM(u.Email)), ''''''''), NULLIF(LTRIM(RTRIM(c.Email)), '''''''')) AS Email,
        COALESCE(NULLIF(LTRIM(RTRIM(c.PhoneNumber)), ''''''''), NULLIF(LTRIM(RTRIM(u.PhoneNumber)), '''''''')) AS Phone,
        l.Name AS LocationName
      FROM dbo.WN_Invoices i WITH (NOLOCK)
      LEFT JOIN dbo.WN_Bookings b WITH (NOLOCK) ON b.Id = i.BookingId
      LEFT JOIN dbo.WN_Users u WITH (NOLOCK) ON u.Id = i.UserId
      OUTER APPLY (SELECT TOP 1 cc.Code, cc.Company, cc.FirstName, cc.LastName, cc.Email, cc.PhoneNumber
                     FROM dbo.WN_Customers cc WITH (NOLOCK)
                    WHERE ((b.CustomerCode IS NOT NULL AND b.CustomerCode <> '''''''' AND cc.Code = b.CustomerCode)
                           OR (i.UserId > 0 AND cc.UserId = i.UserId))
                      AND (cc.IsActive = 1 OR cc.IsActive IS NULL)
                    ORDER BY CASE WHEN cc.Code = b.CustomerCode THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) c
      LEFT JOIN dbo.WN_Locations l WITH (NOLOCK) ON l.Id = @LocationId
     WHERE i.Id = @InvoiceId;
END'');

    -- 5. Saves the EzPay answer for a claimed invoice. A failure keeps the error and is retried (up to 5 attempts).
    EXEC(N''CREATE OR ALTER PROCEDURE dbo.WN_OneBill_SetResult
    @InvoiceId       INT,
    @Success         BIT,
    @EzPayInvoiceId  BIGINT        = NULL,
    @EzPayTranAuthId BIGINT        = NULL,
    @Error           NVARCHAR(500) = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
    SET XACT_ABORT ON;
    DECLARE @Updated INT;
    BEGIN TRANSACTION;

    IF @Success = 1
        UPDATE dbo.WN_Invoices
           SET OneBillStatus = 2, OneBillCreatedOn = GETDATE(), OneBillError = NULL,
               EzPayInvoiceId = @EzPayInvoiceId, EzPayTranAuthId = @EzPayTranAuthId
         WHERE Id = @InvoiceId AND OneBillStatus = 1;
    ELSE
        UPDATE dbo.WN_Invoices
           SET OneBillStatus = 3, OneBillError = LEFT(@Error, 500)
         WHERE Id = @InvoiceId AND OneBillStatus = 1;
    SET @Updated = @@ROWCOUNT;

    COMMIT TRANSACTION;
    SELECT @Updated AS Updated;
END'');

    -- 6. Invoices still to send: at a One Bill location, created on/after its start, unpaid, not deleted, not yet
    --    created, attempts left, and not being sent right now.
    EXEC(N''CREATE OR ALTER PROCEDURE dbo.WN_OneBill_GetPending
    @Top          INT = 50,
    @MaxAttempts  INT = 5,
    @StaleMinutes INT = 10
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
    SELECT TOP (@Top) i.Id AS InvoiceId
      FROM dbo.WN_Invoices i WITH (NOLOCK)
      LEFT JOIN dbo.WN_CustomInvoices ci WITH (NOLOCK) ON ci.InvoiceId = i.Id
      LEFT JOIN dbo.WN_Bookings b WITH (NOLOCK) ON b.Id = i.BookingId
      LEFT JOIN dbo.WN_Spaces s WITH (NOLOCK) ON s.Id = b.SpaceId
      LEFT JOIN dbo.WN_Users u WITH (NOLOCK) ON u.Id = i.UserId
      JOIN dbo.WN_Locations l WITH (NOLOCK) ON l.Id = COALESCE(ci.LocationId, s.LocationId, u.LocationId)
     WHERE NULLIF(LTRIM(RTRIM(l.OneBillBillerCode)), '''''''') IS NOT NULL
       AND NULLIF(LTRIM(RTRIM(l.OneBillPrefix)), '''''''') IS NOT NULL
       AND (l.OneBillFrom IS NULL OR i.CreatedOn >= l.OneBillFrom)
       AND ISNULL(i.StatusId, 0) <> 40
       AND ISNULL(i.GrandTotal, 0) > 0
       AND ISNULL(i.PaidTotal, 0) < i.GrandTotal
       AND i.OneBillAttempts < @MaxAttempts
       AND (i.OneBillStatus IS NULL OR i.OneBillStatus = 3
            OR (i.OneBillStatus = 1 AND i.OneBillClaimedOn <= DATEADD(MINUTE, -@StaleMinutes, GETDATE())))
     ORDER BY i.Id;
END'');

    -- 7. The One Bill number of an invoice (only once it exists on EzPay), for the invoice PDF / email / screens.
    EXEC(N''CREATE OR ALTER PROCEDURE dbo.WN_OneBill_GetByInvoice
    @InvoiceId INT
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
    SELECT i.Id AS InvoiceId,
           CASE WHEN i.OneBillStatus = 2 THEN i.OneBillNumber END AS OneBillNumber,
           i.OneBillStatus,
           i.OneBillError
      FROM dbo.WN_Invoices i WITH (NOLOCK)
     WHERE i.Id = @InvoiceId;
END'');

    COMMIT TRANSACTION;
    SELECT ''One Bill installed: WN_Locations / WN_Invoices columns and 4 WN_OneBill_* procedures.'' AS Result;
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
    IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0 ROLLBACK TRANSACTION;
    THROW;
END CATCH;
');

    -- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    -- Step 5: OB_03    One Bill: payment notifications table   (OneBill/OB_03_one_bill_payments.txt)
    SET @Step = N'5: OB_03    One Bill: payment notifications table';
    EXEC(N'-- ============================================================================================
-- One Bill payment notifications: when a customer pays a One Bill invoice, EzPay calls the WorkNest API
-- (POST api/onebill/payment-notification) with { billno, rcvdamount, rcvddate, rcvdtime, rcvdvia }. Every notification
-- is kept in WN_OneBill_Payments, and the payment is recorded on the invoice with dbo.WN_RecordInvoicePayment (PaidTotal,
-- Paid / Partial status, access cards, WN_Payments row), all in one transaction.
-- The API answers EzPay "success" only when the payment was recorded on the invoice (Applied), or is a repeat of a
-- notification that already was (Duplicate). Every other outcome answers an error, so EzPay sends it again; each attempt
-- is kept as a row for review.
-- Needs OB_01_one_bill (WN_Invoices.OneBill* columns) and INV_FIX_A (WN_RecordInvoicePayment).
-- Run as ONE statement in DBeaver: select all (Cmd+A), then Ctrl+Enter. No GO / USE needed.
-- One transaction: if anything fails, nothing is changed. Safe to run again.
-- ============================================================================================
SET XACT_ABORT ON;
BEGIN TRY
    BEGIN TRANSACTION;

    -- 1. Every notification EzPay sends, as received, with what was done with it.
    --    Outcome: Applied (payment recorded on the invoice), Duplicate (repeat of an Applied notification: not recorded
    --    twice), AlreadyPaid (invoice was already fully paid: not recorded), Unmatched (no invoice has this bill number:
    --    not recorded), Invalid (amount missing or not above zero). Only Applied / Duplicate answer EzPay "success".
    IF OBJECT_ID(''dbo.WN_OneBill_Payments'', ''U'') IS NULL
        EXEC(N''CREATE TABLE dbo.WN_OneBill_Payments (
            Id                 INT IDENTITY(1, 1) NOT NULL CONSTRAINT PK_WN_OneBill_Payments PRIMARY KEY,
            BillNo             VARCHAR(30)    NOT NULL,
            RcvdAmount         DECIMAL(18, 2) NOT NULL,
            RcvdDate           DATE           NULL,
            RcvdTime           TIME(0)        NULL,
            RcvdVia            NVARCHAR(50)   NULL,
            RawBody            NVARCHAR(MAX)  NULL,
            RemoteIp           VARCHAR(64)    NULL,
            ReceivedOn         DATETIME2(3)   NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_WN_OneBill_Payments_ReceivedOn DEFAULT (SYSUTCDATETIME()),
            InvoiceId          INT            NULL,
            Outcome            VARCHAR(20)    NOT NULL,
            Message            NVARCHAR(500)  NULL,
            PaidTotalAfter     DECIMAL(18, 4) NULL,
            InvoiceStatusAfter INT            NULL
        );'');
    IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM sys.indexes WHERE name = ''IX_WN_OneBill_Payments_BillNo'' AND object_id = OBJECT_ID(''dbo.WN_OneBill_Payments''))
        EXEC(N''CREATE INDEX IX_WN_OneBill_Payments_BillNo ON dbo.WN_OneBill_Payments (BillNo, RcvdDate, RcvdTime, RcvdAmount);'');
    IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM sys.indexes WHERE name = ''IX_WN_OneBill_Payments_InvoiceId'' AND object_id = OBJECT_ID(''dbo.WN_OneBill_Payments''))
        EXEC(N''CREATE INDEX IX_WN_OneBill_Payments_InvoiceId ON dbo.WN_OneBill_Payments (InvoiceId);'');

    -- 2. Records one notification. One transaction: the notification row and the invoice payment are saved together
    --    or not at all (the API then answers an error and EzPay sends the notification again).
    EXEC(N''CREATE OR ALTER PROCEDURE dbo.WN_OneBill_RecordPayment
    @BillNo     VARCHAR(30),
    @RcvdAmount DECIMAL(18, 2),
    @RcvdDate   DATE          = NULL,
    @RcvdTime   TIME(0)       = NULL,
    @RcvdVia    NVARCHAR(50)  = NULL,
    @RawBody    NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
    @RemoteIp   VARCHAR(64)   = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
    SET XACT_ABORT ON;

    DECLARE @Outcome VARCHAR(20), @Message NVARCHAR(500), @InvoiceId INT, @InvoiceNumber NVARCHAR(50),
            @Due DECIMAL(18, 4), @Paid DECIMAL(18, 4), @Matches INT, @PaidTotalAfter DECIMAL(18, 4), @StatusAfter INT,
            @NotificationId INT;
    SET @BillNo = LTRIM(RTRIM(@BillNo));

    BEGIN TRANSACTION;

    -- Same notification as one already recorded on its invoice (EzPay sends again when it got no answer): record nothing
    -- twice. Earlier attempts that were NOT recorded (Unmatched / AlreadyPaid / Invalid) are tried again in full.
    SELECT TOP 1 @NotificationId = p.Id, @InvoiceId = p.InvoiceId
      FROM dbo.WN_OneBill_Payments p WITH (UPDLOCK, HOLDLOCK)
     WHERE p.BillNo = @BillNo AND p.RcvdAmount = @RcvdAmount
       AND ISNULL(p.RcvdDate, ''''19000101'''') = ISNULL(@RcvdDate, ''''19000101'''')
       AND ISNULL(p.RcvdTime, ''''00:00:00'''') = ISNULL(@RcvdTime, ''''00:00:00'''')
       AND p.Outcome = ''''Applied''''
     ORDER BY p.Id;

    IF @NotificationId IS NOT NULL
    BEGIN
        SET @Outcome = ''''Duplicate'''';
        SET @Message = ''''Same notification as #'''' + CAST(@NotificationId AS VARCHAR(12)) + '''', already recorded on the invoice; not recorded again.'''';
        SELECT @PaidTotalAfter = PaidTotalAfter, @StatusAfter = InvoiceStatusAfter FROM dbo.WN_OneBill_Payments WHERE Id = @NotificationId;
        SELECT @InvoiceNumber = InvoiceNumber FROM dbo.WN_Invoices WHERE Id = @InvoiceId;
    END
    ELSE IF ISNULL(@RcvdAmount, 0) <= 0
    BEGIN
        SET @Outcome = ''''Invalid'''';
        SET @Message = ''''rcvdamount must be greater than 0.'''';
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        -- The invoice: EzPay sends our OneBillRef (e.g. 103400000459) as billno. The full One Bill number, or its last 12+
        -- digits (biller code + invoice Id), are matched as well in case a bank sends that instead.
        SELECT @Matches = COUNT(*), @InvoiceId = MIN(i.Id)
          FROM dbo.WN_Invoices i
         WHERE i.OneBillNumber IS NOT NULL
           AND (i.OneBillNumber = @BillNo OR i.OneBillRef = @BillNo
                OR (LEN(@BillNo) >= 12 AND RIGHT(i.OneBillNumber, LEN(@BillNo)) = @BillNo));

        IF ISNULL(@Matches, 0) <> 1
        BEGIN
            SET @InvoiceId = NULL;
            SET @Outcome = ''''Unmatched'''';
            SET @Message = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@Matches, 0) = 0 THEN ''''No invoice has this bill number.''''
                                ELSE ''''More than one invoice matches this bill number.'''' END;
        END
        ELSE
        BEGIN
            SELECT @InvoiceNumber = i.InvoiceNumber,
                   @Due = i.GrandTotal - ISNULL(i.WHTAmount, 0),
                   @Paid = ISNULL(i.PaidTotal, 0)
              FROM dbo.WN_Invoices i WHERE i.Id = @InvoiceId;

            IF @Paid >= @Due
            BEGIN
                SET @Outcome = ''''AlreadyPaid'''';
                SET @Message = ''''Invoice '''' + @InvoiceNumber + '''' was already fully paid; payment not recorded (check for a double payment).'''';
            END
            ELSE
            BEGIN
                -- Same procedure as Record Payment in the CMS: PaidTotal, Paid / Partial, access cards, WN_Payments row.
                DECLARE @Result TABLE (IsSuccessful INT, Message NVARCHAR(200), PaidTotal DECIMAL(18, 4), StatusId INT);
                DECLARE @Ref NVARCHAR(100) = N''''1BILL-'''' + @BillNo
                    + ISNULL(N''''-'''' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(8), @RcvdDate, 112), N'''''''')
                    + ISNULL(N''''-'''' + REPLACE(CONVERT(NVARCHAR(8), @RcvdTime, 108), N'''':'''', N''''''''), N'''''''');
                DECLARE @Notes NVARCHAR(MAX) = N''''Paid with 1Bill (EzPay)''''
                    + ISNULL(N'''' via '''' + NULLIF(LTRIM(RTRIM(@RcvdVia)), N''''''''), N'''''''')
                    + ISNULL(N'''' on '''' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(10), @RcvdDate, 23), N'''''''')
                    + ISNULL(N'''' '''' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(8), @RcvdTime, 108), N'''''''')
                    + N'''', bill no '''' + @BillNo + N''''.'''';

                INSERT INTO @Result (IsSuccessful, Message, PaidTotal, StatusId)
                EXEC dbo.WN_RecordInvoicePayment
                     @InvoiceId = @InvoiceId, @PaidAmount = @RcvdAmount, @PaymentMethod = N''''1Bill'''',
                     @TransactionRef = @Ref, @Notes = @Notes;

                SELECT TOP 1 @PaidTotalAfter = PaidTotal, @StatusAfter = StatusId FROM @Result;
                IF @PaidTotalAfter IS NULL OR @PaidTotalAfter < @Paid + @RcvdAmount
                    THROW 50010, N''''WN_RecordInvoicePayment did not record the payment.'''', 1;   -- rolls everything back; API answers an error
                SET @Outcome = ''''Applied'''';
                SET @Message = ''''Payment of '''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(30), @RcvdAmount) + '''' recorded on invoice '''' + @InvoiceNumber
                    + CASE WHEN @PaidTotalAfter < @Due THEN '''' (part payment).''''
                           WHEN @PaidTotalAfter > @Due THEN '''' (more than the amount due).''''
                           ELSE ''''.'''' END;

                -- A payment proves the invoice is on EzPay, even if our call had been recorded as failed.
                UPDATE dbo.WN_Invoices
                   SET OneBillStatus = 2, OneBillCreatedOn = ISNULL(OneBillCreatedOn, GETDATE()), OneBillError = NULL
                 WHERE Id = @InvoiceId AND ISNULL(OneBillStatus, 0) <> 2;
            END;
        END;
    END;

    INSERT INTO dbo.WN_OneBill_Payments
        (BillNo, RcvdAmount, RcvdDate, RcvdTime, RcvdVia, RawBody, RemoteIp, InvoiceId, Outcome, Message, PaidTotalAfter, InvoiceStatusAfter)
    VALUES
        (@BillNo, ISNULL(@RcvdAmount, 0), @RcvdDate, @RcvdTime, @RcvdVia, @RawBody, @RemoteIp, @InvoiceId, @Outcome, LEFT(@Message, 500),
         @PaidTotalAfter, @StatusAfter);
    SET @NotificationId = SCOPE_IDENTITY();

    COMMIT TRANSACTION;

    SELECT @NotificationId AS NotificationId, @Outcome AS Outcome, @Message AS Message, @InvoiceId AS InvoiceId,
           @InvoiceNumber AS InvoiceNumber, @PaidTotalAfter AS PaidTotal, @StatusAfter AS StatusId;
END'');

    COMMIT TRANSACTION;
    SELECT ''One Bill payments installed: WN_OneBill_Payments table and WN_OneBill_RecordPayment.'' AS Result;
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
    IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0 ROLLBACK TRANSACTION;
    THROW;
END CATCH;
');

    -- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    -- Step 6: OB_04    One Bill: WN_OneBill_RecordPayment with @StatusCode   (OneBill/OB_04_record_payment_status_code.txt)
    SET @Step = N'6: OB_04    One Bill: WN_OneBill_RecordPayment with @StatusCode';
    EXEC(N'-- ============================================================================================
-- OB_04: dbo.WN_OneBill_RecordPayment with the @StatusCode interface (ezpay* parameters, @StatusCode OUTPUT, app lock),
-- plus what it needs to be safe with EzPay, which sends a notification again whenever it gets no answer:
--   * every path commits or rolls back (before, a non-200 path left the transaction open: error 266, lock held);
--   * the same notification twice is not recorded twice (it was: one payment marked the invoice paid twice);
--   * a payment on an invoice that is already fully paid, or with no matching invoice, is kept for review, not recorded.
-- The bill number is matched on WN_Invoices.OneBillNumber (the 1-Link consumer number) or OneBillRef (what we send EzPay).
-- @StatusCode: 200 recorded on the invoice; 201 the same notification was already recorded (nothing done);
--              202 invoice already fully paid (not recorded); 104 no invoice has this bill number (not recorded);
--              105 amount missing or not above zero; 103 database error (rolled back, EzPay should send it again).
-- The API answers EzPay success for 200 and 201 only.
-- Every notification is kept in dbo.WN_OneBill_Payments (OB_03), whatever its outcome.
-- Run as ONE statement in DBeaver (select all, then Ctrl+Enter). One transaction. Safe to run again.
-- ============================================================================================
SET XACT_ABORT ON;
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON;
SET ANSI_NULLS ON;
BEGIN TRY
    IF OBJECT_ID(''dbo.WN_OneBill_Payments'', ''U'') IS NULL
        THROW 50001, N''dbo.WN_OneBill_Payments is missing: run OB_03_one_bill_payments.txt first. Nothing changed.'', 1;

    BEGIN TRANSACTION;

    EXEC(N''CREATE OR ALTER PROCEDURE dbo.WN_OneBill_RecordPayment
    @ezpayRefNo      NVARCHAR(30),
    @ezpayRcvdAmount DECIMAL(18, 2),
    @ezpayRcvdDate   DATE          = NULL,
    @ezpayRcvdTime   TIME          = NULL,
    @ezpayRcvdVia    NVARCHAR(50)  = NULL,
    @StatusCode      INT OUTPUT,             -- 200 recorded, 201 already recorded, 202 invoice already paid, 104 no invoice, 105 bad amount, 103 db error
    @RawBody         NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
    @RemoteIp        VARCHAR(64)   = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET XACT_ABORT, NOCOUNT ON;

    DECLARE @Outcome VARCHAR(20), @Message NVARCHAR(500), @InvoiceId INT, @InvoiceNumber NVARCHAR(50), @Matches INT,
            @Due DECIMAL(18, 4), @Paid DECIMAL(18, 4), @PaidTotalAfter DECIMAL(18, 4), @StatusAfter INT,
            @Ref NVARCHAR(100), @Notes NVARCHAR(MAX), @NotificationId INT, @returnCode INT;
    DECLARE @Result TABLE (IsSuccessful INT, [Message] NVARCHAR(MAX), PaidTotal DECIMAL(18, 4), StatusId INT);

    SET @StatusCode = 200;
    SET @ezpayRefNo = LTRIM(RTRIM(@ezpayRefNo));

    BEGIN TRY
        BEGIN TRANSACTION;

        -- One notification at a time (EzPay can send the same one again while the first is still running).
        EXEC @returnCode = sp_getapplock @Resource = ''''WN_OneBill_RecordPayment'''', @LockMode = ''''Exclusive'''', @LockOwner = ''''Transaction'''';
        IF @returnCode < 0
            THROW 50020, N''''Could not lock WN_OneBill_RecordPayment.'''', 1;

        IF ISNULL(@ezpayRcvdAmount, 0) <= 0
        BEGIN
            SELECT @StatusCode = 105, @Outcome = ''''Invalid'''', @Message = ''''Amount missing or not above zero.'''';
        END
        ELSE IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM dbo.WN_OneBill_Payments p
                         WHERE p.BillNo = @ezpayRefNo AND p.RcvdAmount = @ezpayRcvdAmount AND p.Outcome = ''''Applied''''
                           AND ISNULL(p.RcvdDate, ''''19000101'''') = ISNULL(@ezpayRcvdDate, ''''19000101'''')
                           AND ISNULL(p.RcvdTime, ''''00:00:00'''') = ISNULL(CAST(@ezpayRcvdTime AS TIME(0)), ''''00:00:00''''))
        BEGIN
            -- Same notification as one already recorded: nothing to do (EzPay just did not get our first answer).
            SELECT TOP 1 @InvoiceId = p.InvoiceId, @PaidTotalAfter = p.PaidTotalAfter, @StatusAfter = p.InvoiceStatusAfter
              FROM dbo.WN_OneBill_Payments p
             WHERE p.BillNo = @ezpayRefNo AND p.RcvdAmount = @ezpayRcvdAmount AND p.Outcome = ''''Applied''''
             ORDER BY p.Id;
            SELECT @StatusCode = 201, @Outcome = ''''Duplicate'''', @Message = ''''Same notification as one already recorded; not recorded again.'''';
        END
        ELSE
        BEGIN
            -- The invoice: the 1-Link consumer number (OneBillNumber) or our ref (OneBillRef).
            SELECT @Matches = COUNT(*), @InvoiceId = MIN(i.Id)
              FROM dbo.WN_Invoices i
             WHERE i.OneBillNumber = @ezpayRefNo OR i.OneBillRef = @ezpayRefNo;

            IF ISNULL(@Matches, 0) <> 1
                SELECT @InvoiceId = NULL, @StatusCode = 104, @Outcome = ''''Unmatched'''',
                       @Message = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@Matches, 0) = 0 THEN ''''No invoice has this bill number.'''' ELSE ''''More than one invoice has this bill number.'''' END;
            ELSE
            BEGIN
                SELECT @InvoiceNumber = i.InvoiceNumber,
                       @Due  = ISNULL(i.GrandTotal, 0) - ISNULL(i.WHTAmount, 0),
                       @Paid = ISNULL(i.PaidTotal, 0)
                  FROM dbo.WN_Invoices i WITH (UPDLOCK, HOLDLOCK)
                 WHERE i.Id = @InvoiceId;

                IF @Paid >= @Due
                    SELECT @StatusCode = 202, @Outcome = ''''AlreadyPaid'''',
                           @Message = ''''Invoice '''' + @InvoiceNumber + '''' was already fully paid; payment not recorded (check for a double payment).'''';
                ELSE
                BEGIN
                    SET @Ref = N''''1BILL-'''' + @ezpayRefNo
                             + ISNULL(N''''-'''' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(8), @ezpayRcvdDate, 112), N'''''''')
                             + ISNULL(N''''-'''' + REPLACE(CONVERT(NVARCHAR(8), @ezpayRcvdTime, 108), N'''':'''', N''''''''), N'''''''');
                    SET @Notes = N''''Paid with 1Bill (EzPay)''''
                             + ISNULL(N'''' via '''' + NULLIF(LTRIM(RTRIM(@ezpayRcvdVia)), N''''''''), N'''''''')
                             + ISNULL(N'''' on '''' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(10), @ezpayRcvdDate, 23), N'''''''')
                             + ISNULL(N'''' '''' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(8), @ezpayRcvdTime, 108), N'''''''')
                             + N'''', bill no '''' + @ezpayRefNo + N''''.'''';

                    INSERT INTO @Result (IsSuccessful, [Message], PaidTotal, StatusId)
                    EXEC dbo.WN_RecordInvoicePayment
                         @InvoiceId = @InvoiceId, @PaidAmount = @ezpayRcvdAmount, @PaymentMethod = N''''1Bill'''',
                         @TransactionRef = @Ref, @Notes = @Notes;

                    SELECT TOP 1 @PaidTotalAfter = PaidTotal, @StatusAfter = StatusId FROM @Result;
                    IF @PaidTotalAfter IS NULL OR @PaidTotalAfter < @Paid + @ezpayRcvdAmount
                        THROW 50010, N''''WN_RecordInvoicePayment did not record the payment.'''', 1;   -- all rolled back, 103

                    SET @Outcome = ''''Applied'''';
                    SET @Message = ''''Payment of '''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(30), @ezpayRcvdAmount) + '''' recorded on invoice '''' + @InvoiceNumber
                        + CASE WHEN @PaidTotalAfter < @Due THEN '''' (part payment).''''
                               WHEN @PaidTotalAfter > @Due THEN '''' (more than the amount due).''''
                               ELSE ''''.'''' END;

                    -- A payment proves the invoice is on EzPay, even if our call had been recorded as failed.
                    UPDATE dbo.WN_Invoices
                       SET OneBillStatus = 2, OneBillCreatedOn = ISNULL(OneBillCreatedOn, GETDATE()), OneBillError = NULL
                     WHERE Id = @InvoiceId AND ISNULL(OneBillStatus, 0) <> 2;
                END;
            END;
        END;

        -- Every notification is kept, whatever its outcome (rows that were not recorded are for review).
        INSERT INTO dbo.WN_OneBill_Payments
            (BillNo, RcvdAmount, RcvdDate, RcvdTime, RcvdVia, RawBody, RemoteIp, InvoiceId, Outcome, [Message], PaidTotalAfter, InvoiceStatusAfter)
        VALUES
            (LEFT(@ezpayRefNo, 30), ISNULL(@ezpayRcvdAmount, 0), @ezpayRcvdDate, CAST(@ezpayRcvdTime AS TIME(0)), @ezpayRcvdVia, @RawBody, @RemoteIp,
             @InvoiceId, @Outcome, LEFT(@Message, 500), @PaidTotalAfter, @StatusAfter);
        SET @NotificationId = SCOPE_IDENTITY();

        COMMIT TRANSACTION;

        SELECT @NotificationId AS NotificationId, @StatusCode AS StatusCode, @Outcome AS Outcome, @Message AS Message,
               @InvoiceId AS InvoiceId, @InvoiceNumber AS InvoiceNumber, @PaidTotalAfter AS PaidTotal, @StatusAfter AS StatusId;
    END TRY
    BEGIN CATCH
        SET @StatusCode = 103;   -- database error: nothing kept, EzPay should send it again
        IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0 ROLLBACK TRANSACTION;
        THROW;
    END CATCH;
END'');

    COMMIT TRANSACTION;
    SELECT ''OB_04 installed: WN_OneBill_RecordPayment with @StatusCode.'' AS Result;
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
    IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0 ROLLBACK TRANSACTION;
    THROW;
END CATCH;
');

    -- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    -- Step 7: RC_01    monthly split of invoices (WN_FeeReceipt_MonthPlan)   (FeeReceipts/RC_01_month_plan.txt)
    SET @Step = N'7: RC_01    monthly split of invoices (WN_FeeReceipt_MonthPlan)';
    EXEC(N'-- ============================================================================================
-- Fee Receipts per month: dbo.WN_FeeReceipt_MonthPlan(@InvoiceId) splits a multi-month invoice (the first / advance
-- invoice that bills several months at once) into one row per calendar month, so one fee receipt can be issued per month.
-- Run as ONE statement in DBeaver: select all (Cmd+A), then Ctrl+Enter. No GO / USE needed.
-- One transaction: if anything fails, nothing is changed. Safe to run again (CREATE OR ALTER). Changes no data.
--
-- Adds:
--   dbo.WN_FeeReceipt_MonthPlan(@InvoiceId INT) - table-valued function, one row per receipt month:
--     MonthNo / MonthCount           1..n, n = number of receipts for the invoice.
--     BillingMonth                   1st day of the calendar month the receipt is for.
--     PeriodFrom / PeriodTo          the days that month covers.
--     RentGross, Discount, Service, Tax, Deposit, WHT
--     Payable                        what the customer pays for that month = RentGross - Discount + Tax + Deposit
--                                    (WHT is withheld by the customer, so it is not in Payable).
--     Revenue                        Payable + WHT - Deposit.
--   SUM(Payable) = GrandTotal - WHTAmount, SUM(WHT) = WHTAmount, SUM(Deposit) = SecurityDepositAmount,
--   SUM(Revenue) = GrandTotal - SecurityDepositAmount - exactly, for every invoice (to the cent: a few older invoices
--   carry 4 decimals in GrandTotal / TaxTotal; the plan uses those totals rounded to 2 decimals).
--
-- How the months are worked out (from WN_Invoices; SubTotal / RoomRentExclTax are NOT used, they are not reliable):
--   T (customer pays) = GrandTotal - WHTAmount
--   X (tax)           = TaxOnServiceCharges (TaxTotal when that is 0); if that cannot reproduce the months, TaxTotal
--                       (older invoices charged a blended tax on line 1 that only TaxTotal holds)
--   gross rent Rg     = T - X - SecurityDepositAmount + DiscountTotal      (rent incl. service charges, before discount)
--   Months ("segments"):
--     line 1 says "(d days prorated + k Month(s))" -> month 1 = BillingPeriodStart .. end of that month (d days),
--                                                     then k whole calendar months up to BillingPeriodEnd.
--     otherwise                                    -> months anchored on BillingPeriodStart''s day (Nov 6 - Dec 5, ...;
--                                                     calendar months when it starts on the 1st).
--   Monthly rent M:  prorated     M = Rg / (d / div + k), div = 30 (current engine) or the days of the first month
--                                     (old invoices) - whichever gives a whole number;
--                    not prorated M = Rg / number of months.
--   Every whole month: RentGross = M; Discount = M * p% (percentage discount) or DiscountTotal / units (fixed);
--                      Service = ServiceCharges / units; Tax = X / units; WHT = net / (1 - rate) - net, net = M - Discount.
--                      units = months charged service by the engine (the prorated first month counts only when it
--                      starts before the 15th). Fixed discount / service / tax per month are cut to the cent.
--   d is taken from the description (current engine: 31 - start day; old invoices: real days left in the month).
--   Month 1 takes the rest of every total (rent, discount, service, tax, WHT) plus the whole security deposit,
--   so the months always add up to the invoice exactly.
--
-- ONE row (MonthNo = 1, MonthCount = 1, the whole invoice; BillingMonth = month of BillingPeriodStart, else IssuedOn) for:
--   custom invoices (IsCustom = 1 or InvoiceTypeId = 7), capacity overage surcharges, meeting rooms, invoices without
--   BillingPeriodStart / BillingPeriodEnd, periods that are one month or less, and any invoice whose amounts the rules
--   above cannot reproduce exactly (no whole monthly rent, period not made of whole months, a negative month 1, ...).
-- ============================================================================================
SET XACT_ABORT ON;
BEGIN TRY
    BEGIN TRANSACTION;

    EXEC(N''CREATE OR ALTER FUNCTION dbo.WN_FeeReceipt_MonthPlan (@InvoiceId INT)
RETURNS @Plan TABLE (
    MonthNo      INT,
    MonthCount   INT,
    BillingMonth DATE,
    PeriodFrom   DATE,
    PeriodTo     DATE,
    RentGross    DECIMAL(18, 2),
    Discount     DECIMAL(18, 2),
    Service      DECIMAL(18, 2),
    Tax          DECIMAL(18, 2),
    Deposit      DECIMAL(18, 2),
    WHT          DECIMAL(18, 2),
    Payable      DECIMAL(18, 2),
    Revenue      DECIMAL(18, 2)
)
AS
BEGIN
    DECLARE @PS DATE, @PE DATE, @IssuedOn DATE, @N INT, @IsCustom BIT, @TypeId INT, @BookingId INT,
            @G DECIMAL(19, 4), @Wraw DECIMAL(19, 4), @Rate DECIMAL(18, 6), @Dep DECIMAL(18, 2), @D DECIMAL(18, 2),
            @S DECIMAL(18, 2), @X1 DECIMAL(19, 4), @X2 DECIMAL(19, 4), @Line1 NVARCHAR(500);

    SELECT @PS = CONVERT(DATE, i.BillingPeriodStart), @PE = CONVERT(DATE, i.BillingPeriodEnd), @IssuedOn = i.IssuedOn,
           @N = i.AdvanceRentMonths, @IsCustom = ISNULL(i.IsCustom, 0), @TypeId = i.InvoiceTypeId, @BookingId = i.BookingId,
           @G = ISNULL(i.GrandTotal, 0), @Wraw = ISNULL(i.WHTAmount, 0), @Rate = ISNULL(i.WHTRate, 0) / 100.0,
           @Dep = ISNULL(i.SecurityDepositAmount, 0), @D = ISNULL(i.DiscountTotal, 0), @S = ISNULL(i.ServiceCharges, 0),
           @X1 = CASE WHEN ISNULL(i.TaxOnServiceCharges, 0) <> 0 THEN i.TaxOnServiceCharges ELSE ISNULL(i.TaxTotal, 0) END,
           @X2 = ISNULL(i.TaxTotal, 0)
    FROM dbo.WN_Invoices i
    WHERE i.Id = @InvoiceId;

    IF @@ROWCOUNT = 0 RETURN;

    SELECT TOP (1) @Line1 = l.Description
    FROM dbo.WN_InvoiceLines l
    WHERE l.InvoiceId = @InvoiceId
    ORDER BY l.SortOrder, l.Id;

    -- Amounts are worked out from the unrounded totals (some older invoices carry 4 decimals) and then rounded.
    -- Tax X is taken as T - Deposit - (Rg - Discount), so Payable always adds up to T to the cent.
    -- Tax: TaxOnServiceCharges (TaxTotal when 0); when that cannot reproduce the months, TaxTotal is tried
    -- (older invoices charged a blended tax on line 1, which only TaxTotal holds).
    DECLARE @T  DECIMAL(18, 2) = ROUND(@G, 2) - ROUND(@Wraw, 2),            -- customer payable
            @W  DECIMAL(18, 2) = ROUND(@Wraw, 2),                           -- WHT
            @Rg DECIMAL(18, 2) = ROUND(@G - @Wraw - @X1 - @Dep + @D, 2),   -- gross rent (incl. service charges, before discount)
            @X  DECIMAL(18, 2), @RgTry DECIMAL(18, 2), @Try INT;
    SET @X = @T - @Dep - @Rg + @D;

    -- ---- Can it be split? -------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    DECLARE @Split BIT = 1;
    IF @IsCustom = 1 OR @TypeId = 7 OR @PS IS NULL OR @PE IS NULL OR @PE < @PS
       OR DATEFROMPARTS(YEAR(@PS), MONTH(@PS), 1) = DATEFROMPARTS(YEAR(@PE), MONTH(@PE), 1)
       OR @Rg <= 0 OR @T < 0 OR @Dep < 0 OR @D < 0 OR @S < 0 OR @X < 0 OR @W < 0
        SET @Split = 0;
    IF @Split = 1 AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM dbo.WN_InvoiceLines l WHERE l.InvoiceId = @InvoiceId
                                AND (l.Description LIKE N''''Capacity Overage Surcharge%''''
                                     OR l.Description LIKE N''''Meeting Room Booking Rent%''''))
        SET @Split = 0;
    IF @Split = 1 AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM dbo.WN_Bookings b
                                JOIN dbo.WN_Spaces s ON s.Id = b.SpaceId
                                JOIN dbo.WN_SpaceTypes st ON st.Id = s.SpaceTypeId
                              WHERE b.Id = @BookingId AND (st.Name LIKE N''''%Meeting%'''' OR st.Description LIKE N''''%Meeting%''''))
        SET @Split = 0;

    -- ---- Months --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    DECLARE @Segs TABLE (No INT PRIMARY KEY, FromD DATE, ToD DATE);
    DECLARE @Prorated BIT = 0, @Days INT, @F INT, @Count INT = 0, @Units INT, @M DECIMAL(18, 2), @Exact DECIMAL(38, 10),
            @Div INT, @Pos INT, @Txt NVARCHAR(500), @i INT;

    IF @Split = 1 AND @Line1 LIKE N''''%([0-9]% days prorated + [0-9]% Month(s))%''''
    BEGIN
        -- "(d days prorated + k Month(s))": d before " days prorated", k after "+ ".
        SET @Pos = CHARINDEX(N'''' days prorated + '''', @Line1);
        SET @Txt = REVERSE(LEFT(@Line1, @Pos - 1));
        SET @Txt = REVERSE(LEFT(@Txt, CHARINDEX(N''''('''', @Txt + N''''('''') - 1));
        SET @Days = TRY_CONVERT(INT, @Txt);
        SET @Txt = SUBSTRING(@Line1, @Pos + LEN(N'''' days prorated +'''') + 1, 20);
        SET @F = TRY_CONVERT(INT, LEFT(@Txt, CHARINDEX(N'''' '''', @Txt + N'''' '''') - 1));

        -- d is days charged for the stub (start .. end of its month; the current engine counts 31 - start day,
        -- older invoices the real days), so it is only checked loosely; the whole monthly rent below is the real test.
        IF @Days IS NULL OR @F IS NULL OR @Days < 0 OR @F <= 0
           OR @Days > DATEDIFF(DAY, @PS, EOMONTH(@PS)) + 1
           OR @PE <> EOMONTH(@PE) OR DATEDIFF(MONTH, @PS, @PE) <> @F
            SET @Split = 0;
        ELSE
        BEGIN
            SET @Prorated = 1;
            INSERT INTO @Segs VALUES (1, @PS, EOMONTH(@PS));
            SET @i = 1;
            WHILE @i <= @F
            BEGIN
                INSERT INTO @Segs VALUES (@i + 1, DATEADD(DAY, 1, EOMONTH(@PS, @i - 1)), EOMONTH(@PS, @i));
                SET @i += 1;
            END
            SET @Count = @F + 1;
            -- Monthly rent: Rg = M * (d / div + F) with M a whole number. Current engine div = 30, old invoices the
            -- stub month''''s days. Tried with tax candidate 1, then candidate 2 (TaxTotal) when it differs.
            SET @M = NULL;
            SET @Try = 1;
            WHILE @Try <= 4 AND @M IS NULL
            BEGIN
                IF @Try <= 2 OR @X2 <> @X1
                BEGIN
                    SET @RgTry = ROUND(@G - @Wraw - CASE WHEN @Try <= 2 THEN @X1 ELSE @X2 END - @Dep + @D, 2);
                    SET @Div = CASE WHEN @Try % 2 = 1 THEN 30 ELSE DAY(EOMONTH(@PS)) END;
                    SET @Exact = CONVERT(DECIMAL(38, 10), @RgTry) * @Div / (@Days + @F * @Div);
                    IF @RgTry > 0 AND ABS(@Exact - ROUND(@Exact, 0)) <= 0.01
                    BEGIN
                        SET @M  = ROUND(@Exact, 0);
                        SET @Rg = @RgTry;
                        SET @X  = @T - @Dep - @Rg + @D;
                    END
                END
                SET @Try += 1;
            END
            IF @M IS NULL SET @Split = 0;
            -- Service / tax / fixed discount: the engine charges the stub a unit only when it starts before the 15th.
            SET @Units = CASE WHEN DAY(@PS) < 15 THEN @F + 1 ELSE @F END;
        END
    END
    ELSE IF @Split = 1
    BEGIN
        -- Months anchored on the start day: PS + (i-1) months .. PS + i months - 1 day; the last ends on PE
        -- (some old invoices end on PS + n months instead of the day before).
        SET @i = 1;
        WHILE @i <= 120 AND DATEADD(DAY, -1, DATEADD(MONTH, @i, @PS)) < @PE AND DATEADD(MONTH, @i, @PS) < @PE
            SET @i += 1;
        IF DATEADD(DAY, -1, DATEADD(MONTH, @i, @PS)) = @PE OR DATEADD(MONTH, @i, @PS) = @PE
        BEGIN
            SET @Count = @i;
            SET @i = 1;
            WHILE @i <= @Count
            BEGIN
                INSERT INTO @Segs VALUES (@i, DATEADD(MONTH, @i - 1, @PS),
                                          CASE WHEN @i = @Count THEN @PE ELSE DATEADD(DAY, -1, DATEADD(MONTH, @i, @PS)) END);
                SET @i += 1;
            END
            SET @F = @Count - 1;
            SET @Units = @Count;
            -- Prefer the tax candidate that gives a whole monthly rent; otherwise candidate 1, rent rounded to the cent.
            IF @X2 <> @X1
            BEGIN
                SET @RgTry = ROUND(@G - @Wraw - @X2 - @Dep + @D, 2);
                SET @Exact = CONVERT(DECIMAL(38, 10), @Rg) / @Count;
                IF ABS(@Exact - ROUND(@Exact, 0)) > 0.01 AND @RgTry > 0
                   AND ABS(CONVERT(DECIMAL(38, 10), @RgTry) / @Count - ROUND(CONVERT(DECIMAL(38, 10), @RgTry) / @Count, 0)) <= 0.01
                BEGIN
                    SET @Rg = @RgTry;
                    SET @X  = @T - @Dep - @Rg + @D;
                END
            END
            SET @M = ROUND(@Rg / @Count, 2);
        END
        ELSE
            SET @Split = 0;
        IF @Count < 2 SET @Split = 0;
    END

    -- ---- Per whole month -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    DECLARE @mDisc DECIMAL(18, 2) = 0, @mServ DECIMAL(18, 2) = 0, @mTax DECIMAL(18, 2) = 0, @mWht DECIMAL(18, 2) = 0,
            @p DECIMAL(18, 2), @Net DECIMAL(18, 2);

    IF @Split = 1
    BEGIN
        IF @Units IS NULL OR @Units <= 0 SET @Split = 0;
    END

    IF @Split = 1
    BEGIN
        IF @D > 0
        BEGIN
            SET @p = ROUND(@D * 100 / @Rg, 2);          -- percentage discount when Rg * p% gives DiscountTotal exactly
            IF ROUND(@Rg * @p / 100, 2) = @D
                SET @mDisc = ROUND(@M * @p / 100, 2);
            ELSE
                SET @mDisc = ROUND(@D / @Units, 2, 1);
        END
        -- Cut to the cent (not rounded up), so month 1 never goes negative on an uneven division.
        SET @mServ = ROUND(@S / @Units, 2, 1);
        SET @mTax  = ROUND(@X / @Units, 2, 1);
        SET @Net   = @M - @mDisc;
        IF @Rate > 0 AND @Rate < 1
            SET @mWht = ROUND(@Net / (1 - @Rate), 2) - @Net;

        -- Month 1 gets the remainder of every total; it must not go negative.
        IF @Rg - @F * @M < 0 OR @D - @F * @mDisc < 0 OR @S - @F * @mServ < 0 OR @X - @F * @mTax < 0
           OR @W - @F * @mWht < 0 OR (@Rg - @F * @M) - (@D - @F * @mDisc) < 0 OR @Net < 0
            SET @Split = 0;
    END

    IF @Split = 0
    BEGIN
        INSERT INTO @Plan
        VALUES (1, 1, DATEFROMPARTS(YEAR(ISNULL(@PS, @IssuedOn)), MONTH(ISNULL(@PS, @IssuedOn)), 1), @PS, @PE,
                @Rg, @D, @S, @X, @Dep, @W, @T, @T + @W - @Dep);
        RETURN;
    END

    INSERT INTO @Plan
    SELECT s.No, @Count, DATEFROMPARTS(YEAR(s.FromD), MONTH(s.FromD), 1), s.FromD, s.ToD,
           v.Rent, v.Disc, v.Serv, v.Tax, v.Dep, v.Wht,
           v.Rent - v.Disc + v.Tax + v.Dep,
           v.Rent - v.Disc + v.Tax + v.Wht
    FROM @Segs s
    CROSS APPLY (SELECT CASE WHEN s.No = 1 THEN @Rg - @F * @M     ELSE @M     END AS Rent,
                        CASE WHEN s.No = 1 THEN @D  - @F * @mDisc ELSE @mDisc END AS Disc,
                        CASE WHEN s.No = 1 THEN @S  - @F * @mServ ELSE @mServ END AS Serv,
                        CASE WHEN s.No = 1 THEN @X  - @F * @mTax  ELSE @mTax  END AS Tax,
                        CASE WHEN s.No = 1 THEN @Dep              ELSE 0      END AS Dep,
                        CASE WHEN s.No = 1 THEN @W  - @F * @mWht  ELSE @mWht  END AS Wht) v;

    RETURN;
END'');

    COMMIT TRANSACTION;
    SELECT ''dbo.WN_FeeReceipt_MonthPlan created/updated.'' AS Result;
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
    IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0 ROLLBACK TRANSACTION;
    THROW;
END CATCH
');

    -- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    -- Step 8: RC_02    one fee receipt per month, vouchers, void   (FeeReceipts/RC_02_monthly_receipts.txt)
    SET @Step = N'8: RC_02    one fee receipt per month, vouchers, void';
    EXEC(N'-- ============================================================================================
-- RC_02: one fee receipt per month for multi-month invoices.
-- The customer pays the whole invoice at once: the receipt made in the CMS is month 1 (its rent share + the whole
-- security deposit) and marks the invoice Paid; months 2..N get their own receipt when each month starts
-- (WN_FeeReceipts_CreateDueMonthly, called by the API''s monthly receipt job), each with its own voucher (cash/bank).
-- Single-month invoices keep one receipt. Receipts made before RC_02 are not changed.
-- Needs RC_01_month_plan (dbo.WN_FeeReceipt_MonthPlan).
--
-- Changes:
--   WN_FeeReceipts: BillingMonth, MonthNo, MonthCount; one active receipt per invoice AND month (was per invoice).
--   WN_FeeReceipts_PostVoucher (new): a receipt''s voucher / ledger / customer ledger, for its own amount.
--   WN_FeeReceipts_Insert: receipt 1 = month 1; posting through WN_FeeReceipts_PostVoucher.
--   WN_FeeReceipts_CreateDueMonthly (new): the monthly receipts that are due.
--   WN_FeeReceipts_Void: voids the whole payment (every monthly receipt) and now also removes their accounting.
--   WN_FeeReceipts_GetList / _GetById: + BillingMonth, MonthNo, MonthCount.
--
-- Run as ONE statement in DBeaver (select all, then Ctrl+Enter), on the test database and on the live database.
-- One transaction: if anything fails, nothing is changed. Safe to run again.
-- ============================================================================================
SET XACT_ABORT ON;
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON;
SET ANSI_NULLS ON;
BEGIN TRY
    IF OBJECT_ID(''dbo.WN_FeeReceipt_MonthPlan'') IS NULL
        THROW 50001, N''dbo.WN_FeeReceipt_MonthPlan is missing: run RC_01_month_plan.txt first. Nothing changed.'', 1;

    BEGIN TRANSACTION;

    IF COL_LENGTH(''dbo.WN_FeeReceipts'', ''BillingMonth'') IS NULL
        EXEC(N''ALTER TABLE dbo.WN_FeeReceipts ADD BillingMonth DATE NULL;'');
    IF COL_LENGTH(''dbo.WN_FeeReceipts'', ''MonthNo'') IS NULL
        EXEC(N''ALTER TABLE dbo.WN_FeeReceipts ADD MonthNo INT NULL;'');
    IF COL_LENGTH(''dbo.WN_FeeReceipts'', ''MonthCount'') IS NULL
        EXEC(N''ALTER TABLE dbo.WN_FeeReceipts ADD MonthCount INT NULL;'');

    -- One active receipt per invoice and month (MonthNo NULL = a receipt from before RC_02, one per invoice as before).
    IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM sys.indexes WHERE object_id = OBJECT_ID(''dbo.WN_FeeReceipts'') AND name = ''UQ_WN_FeeReceipts_InvoiceMonth_Active'')
        EXEC(N''CREATE UNIQUE NONCLUSTERED INDEX UQ_WN_FeeReceipts_InvoiceMonth_Active ON dbo.WN_FeeReceipts (InvoiceIdGUID, MonthNo) WHERE [Status] = (1);'');
    IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM sys.indexes WHERE object_id = OBJECT_ID(''dbo.WN_FeeReceipts'') AND name = ''UQ_WN_FeeReceipts_InvoiceIdGUID_Active'')
        EXEC(N''DROP INDEX UQ_WN_FeeReceipts_InvoiceIdGUID_Active ON dbo.WN_FeeReceipts;'');

    -- WN_FeeReceipts_PostVoucher
    EXEC(N''CREATE OR ALTER PROCEDURE dbo.WN_FeeReceipts_PostVoucher
    @ReceiptId   INT,
    @CreatedById INT,
    @TransDate   DATETIME
AS
BEGIN
    -- RC_02: the accounting of ONE fee receipt (Customer Invoice Receipt voucher, TypeId 27), for that receipt''''s own amount:
    -- Cr Accounts Receivable = amount + WHT, Dr Cash/Bank = amount, Dr WHT = WHT (WHT invoices), and the customer ledger
    -- credit. Same rules as the posting WN_FeeReceipts_Insert did inline before RC_02 (moved here unchanged, so the first
    -- receipt and the monthly receipts post the same way). Runs inside the caller''''s transaction; one of its own as well.
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
    SET XACT_ABORT ON;

    DECLARE
        @InvoiceId           INT,
        @InvoiceNumber       NVARCHAR(50),
        @InvoiceTypeId       INT,
        @InvWHTAmount        DECIMAL(18,4),
        @WHTRate             DECIMAL(9,4),
        @BookingId           INT,
        @UserId              INT,
        @LocationId          INT,
        @CompanyId           INT,
        @BranchId            INT,
        @CashInHandAccountId INT,
        @Code                VARCHAR(50),
        @NetTotal            DECIMAL(18,4),
        @CalcWHTAmount       DECIMAL(18,4),
        @Total               DECIMAL(18,4),
        @IsWhtInvoice        BIT,
        @VoucherId           INT = NULL,
        @ReceiptTransDateTime DATETIME = @TransDate;

    BEGIN TRY
        BEGIN TRANSACTION;

        SELECT
            @InvoiceId           = i.Id,
            @InvoiceNumber       = i.InvoiceNumber,
            @InvoiceTypeId       = ISNULL(i.InvoiceTypeId, 1),
            @InvWHTAmount        = ISNULL(i.WHTAmount, 0),
            @WHTRate             = ISNULL(i.WHTRate, 0),
            @BookingId           = i.BookingId,
            @UserId              = i.UserId,
            @CompanyId           = fr.CompanyId,
            @BranchId            = fr.BranchId,
            @CashInHandAccountId = fr.CashInHandAccountId,
            @Code                = fr.Code,
            @NetTotal            = ROUND(ISNULL(fr.TotalAmount, 0), 2),
            @CalcWHTAmount       = ROUND(ISNULL(fr.WHTAmount, 0), 2)
        FROM dbo.WN_FeeReceipts fr WITH (UPDLOCK, HOLDLOCK)
        JOIN dbo.WN_Invoices i WITH (NOLOCK) ON i.PublicId = fr.InvoiceIdGUID
        WHERE fr.Id = @ReceiptId AND fr.Status = 1;

        IF @InvoiceId IS NULL
            RAISERROR(''''Fee receipt not found or void.'''', 16, 2);
        IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM dbo.WN_FeeReceipts WITH (NOLOCK) WHERE Id = @ReceiptId AND VoucherId IS NOT NULL)
            RAISERROR(''''Fee receipt already has a voucher.'''', 16, 1);

        -- The receipt''''s own figures: amount received (net of WHT) + WHT = what Accounts Receivable is credited.
        SET @IsWhtInvoice = CASE WHEN @InvoiceTypeId = 6 OR @InvWHTAmount > 0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END;
        IF @IsWhtInvoice = 0 SET @CalcWHTAmount = 0;
        SET @Total = @NetTotal + @CalcWHTAmount;

        -- Location: booking space''''s location, else the user''''s location (same rule as the other fee receipt procedures).
        SELECT @LocationId = ISNULL(s.LocationId, u.LocationId)
        FROM (SELECT 1 AS One) z
        LEFT JOIN dbo.WN_Bookings b WITH (NOLOCK) ON b.Id = @BookingId
        LEFT JOIN dbo.WN_Spaces s WITH (NOLOCK) ON s.Id = b.SpaceId
        LEFT JOIN dbo.WN_Users u WITH (NOLOCK) ON u.Id = @UserId;

        -- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        -- 14. GL POSTING (Vouchers_Insert, Ledgers_Insert, CustomerLedger_Insert)
        -- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

        -- (a) Resolve Account Receivable AccountId:
        -- Exact COALESCE(st.AccountReceivableId, s.AccountReceivableId, i.AccountReceivableId)
        -- over WN_Invoices -> WN_Bookings -> WN_Spaces -> WN_SpaceTypes
        DECLARE @ARAccountId INT = NULL;

        SELECT TOP 1 
            @ARAccountId = COALESCE(st.AccountReceivableId, s.AccountReceivableId, i.AccountReceivableId)
        FROM dbo.WN_Invoices i WITH (NOLOCK)
        LEFT JOIN dbo.WN_Bookings b WITH (NOLOCK) ON b.Id = i.BookingId
        LEFT JOIN dbo.WN_Spaces s WITH (NOLOCK) ON s.Id = b.SpaceId
        LEFT JOIN dbo.WN_SpaceTypes st WITH (NOLOCK) ON st.Id = s.SpaceTypeId
        WHERE i.Id = @InvoiceId;

        IF @ARAccountId IS NULL OR @ARAccountId <= 0
        BEGIN
            RAISERROR(''''Cannot resolve Accounts Receivable GL account for invoice %s.'''', 16, 1, @InvoiceNumber);
        END

        -- Validate cash account
        IF @CashInHandAccountId IS NULL OR @CashInHandAccountId <= 0
        BEGIN
            RAISERROR(''''Selected Cash/Bank account is invalid.'''', 16, 1);
        END

        -- (b) Resolve WHAccountId via booking''''s WHTaxId -> WN_WHTaxRate
        DECLARE @WHAccountId INT = NULL;
        IF @IsWhtInvoice = 1
        BEGIN
            DECLARE @BkRateId INT = NULL;
            IF @BookingId IS NOT NULL
            BEGIN
                SELECT TOP 1 @BkRateId = TRY_CAST(WHTaxId AS INT)
                FROM dbo.WN_Bookings WITH (NOLOCK)
                WHERE Id = @BookingId;
            END

            IF @BkRateId IS NOT NULL AND @BkRateId > 0
            BEGIN
                SELECT TOP 1 @WHAccountId = NULLIF(WHAccountId, 0)
                FROM dbo.WN_WHTaxRate WITH (NOLOCK)
                WHERE Id = @BkRateId;
            END

            IF (@WHAccountId IS NULL OR @WHAccountId <= 0) AND @WHTRate > 0
            BEGIN
                SELECT TOP 1 @WHAccountId = NULLIF(WHAccountId, 0)
                FROM dbo.WN_WHTaxRate WITH (NOLOCK)
                WHERE WHRate = @WHTRate
